> 📅 **Compiled 25 Oct 2026 · Refreshed 12 Nov 2026, 4:35 PM IST**  
> This file is the **complete index** — every open tech opening found for the 2028 batch, including full-time roles, internships, PPO/PPI tracks and hackathons. For a **shorter, apply-now brief** built only around internships and hackathons, see [2028 Batch — Internships & Hackathons](../All_2028_Batch_Internships_Hackathons_India_2026-11-12/).

## 📌 Snapshot

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Scope | **Every currently-open tech opening in India that a 2028-batch B.Tech (CS) student can apply to today** |
| Compiled on | **25 Oct 2026** |
| Refreshed on | **12 Nov 2026**, 4:35 PM IST |
| Total openings indexed | **2536** across **6 sources** |
| Corporate roles open to pre-final-year / fresher students | **78** |
| Openings with a stated PPO / PPI pathway | **4** |
| Entry-level full-time roles (no batch gate) | **740** |
| Corporate internships (no batch gate) | **751** |
| Aggregator internships (myinternships.in + Unstop) | **555** |
| Training-institute / staffing-agency openings | **14** |
| Open hackathons & competitions | **394** — Devpost 187, MLH 23, Unstop 184 |
| Openings confirmed live on 12 Nov 2026 | **2536** of 2536 |
| LinkedIn sweep | 174 fresh queries · 2,903 unique job IDs returned · **every one of the 1587 postings used in this brief was re-opened individually and checked on 12 Nov 2026** |
| Other sources | Unstop public opportunity API · Devpost JSON API · MLH events API · myinternships.in API |

> 🔍 **The headline finding of this refresh.** On 25 Oct 2026 there was exactly **one** posting that named the **2028 batch** — a St. Xavier's College (Gurugram) intern-analyst posting made on behalf of Bain & Company. **It has since expired.** As of 12 Nov 2026, **zero live postings anywhere name the 2028 batch**, and that is the correct result, not a gap in the research: **product companies do not publish campus postings for a batch that has not started recruiting.** The 2028 campus cycle opens around **August 2027**. What a 2028-batch student can apply to *today* is **internships, PPO/PPI tracks, pre-final-year roles and hackathons** — which is what this brief is built around.

> ⚠️ **The other number worth knowing.** The median fresher package in India is **₹3.0–4.5 LPA** (AmbitionBox / Glassdoor / LinkedIn salary aggregations). Mass-recruit drives (TCS, Infosys, Wipro, Cognizant, Accenture) pay **₹3.5–9 LPA**. For a 2028 batch, **the money is not in the mass-recruit drives at all** — it is in landing a good internship with a **PPO** during 3rd year.

---

## 📋 Eligible Batches & Fit

> **Honest fit assessment.** A 2028-batch student joined in **August 2024** and is in **3rd year / 6th semester** in November 2026, graduating **May/June 2028**. That places them in the single best position of the whole cycle: **eligible for every internship posted today**, and **eligible for the 2027–28 campus cycle** that opens in about nine months.

| Batch (Graduation Year) | Fit | Why |
|---|---|---|
| **2024** | ❌ Not eligible | Degree already completed |
| **2025** | ❌ Not eligible | Degree already completed |
| **2026** | ❌ Not eligible | Already graduated |
| **2027** | ⚠️ Different cycle | That batch is in its final year and recruits **now**. Nothing in this brief is aimed at them — see the [2027-batch brief](../All_2027_Batch_Openings_India_BTechCS_2026-10-25/) |
| **2028** | ✅ **Primary target — and the only batch these are for** | Every row below is open to a 2028 graduate: internships and pre-final-year roles **today**, then the campus cycle from ~Aug 2027 |
| **2029** | ⚠️ Partial | 1st/2nd year. **Internships and hackathons yes** (both are open to 1st and 2nd years); fresher full-time roles **no** — those need a degree |

**The 2028-batch window, month by month:**

| When | What is open to you |
|---|---|
| **Nov 2026 – Apr 2027** (now) | **Internships** (3rd year is the sweet spot), hackathons, pre-final-year roles, fresher roles that accept *no experience*. This is the highest-value window and the most crowded |
| **May – Jul 2027** | Final-year internships with a PPO. **This is the decision that sets your package** |
| **Aug – Dec 2027** | The **2028 campus cycle opens.** Campus drives, pre-placement offers, and graduate-trainee tracks become the main event |
| **Jan – Jun 2028** | Final-year internships, PPO conversion, and the fallbacks (mass-recruit) |

---

## 📋 Eligible Branches / Disciplines

> **Honest fit assessment.** Above the ₹4 LPA line the branch filter is real but wider than it looks. Internships hire by **skills and discipline family**, not a hard branch list, and several of the richest tracks in this brief (embedded, VLSI, chip design, computer vision) are ECE/EE roles that a CS student cannot take.

| Branch / Discipline | Fit | Why |
|---|---|---|
| **Computer Science (CS / CSE)** | ✅ **Strongest** | Software, backend, full-stack, data, ML and platform roles dominate every tier here |
| **Information Technology (IT)** | ✅ Strong | Indistinguishable from CS for these roles; accepted in the standard “B.Tech / BCA / MCA” eligibility lines |
| **Artificial Intelligence (AI)** | ✅ Strong | AI/ML new-grad and research internships carry the strongest stipends and the best PPO conversion |
| **Machine Learning (ML)** | ✅ Strong | ML internships, data-science and applied-AI roles; several list AI / ML / Data Science as the accepted degree |
| **Data Science (DS)** | ✅ Strong | Dedicated data / analytics internships across the aggregator and corporate sets |
| **Electronics & Communication (ECE)** | ✅ **Genuinely strong here** | Embedded, VLSI, chip-design and systems internships are a separate, well-paid market |
| **Electrical Engineering (EE)** | ⚠️ Possible | Silicon, embedded, VLSI and ASIC tracks only |
| **Mathematics (Math)** | ⚠️ Possible | Quant, actuarial and applied-research internships |

> 📌 Each row carries its **stated qualification** or **stated category** verbatim. Where a listing is silent on branch, CGPA or backlog, the row says so rather than guessing.

---

## 🧭 How To Read This Brief

| Label | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **live (HTTP 200)** | The posting was opened on 12 Nov 2026 and still renders the job itself |
| **live today (fresh sweep)** | Returned by LinkedIn's live search API during today's sweep |
| **bot-guarded** | The site answers non-browser requests with 403/429 — it opens normally in a browser. Not a dead link |
| **expired** | The posting was live on 25 Oct 2026 but has since been taken down. **Removed from the tables below.** |
| **Deadline** | The date the listing itself states. **Hackers close these early** — treat every date as a real cut-off |
| **PPO / PPI** | The listing states a pre-placement offer / pre-placement internship pathway |
| **Stipend** | Reproduced exactly as the listing states it. “Not stated” means the listing says nothing |

---

## 🔄 What Changed Since 25 Oct 2026

> This refresh is not a cosmetic update. Every LinkedIn link that survived in the 25 Oct 2026 version was **re-opened on 12 Nov 2026**, and 168 of them turned out to be dead — LinkedIn answers an expired posting with HTTP 200 and then quietly redirects to a related-search page, so a naive status check calls a dead link “live”. Those 168 rows have been **removed**, and the count is the reason the numbers moved.

| Change | 25 Oct 2026 | 12 Nov 2026 |
|---|---|---|
| Total openings indexed | 713 | **2536** |
| Hackathons listed | 67 | **394** |
| Postings naming the 2028 batch | 1 | **0** (the one expired) |
| LinkedIn IDs re-opened and checked | 0 | **1,794** |
| Dead links found and removed | 0 | **168** |

## 🎯 Tier 1 — Explicit 2028-Batch Openings

> Postings that name the **2028 batch** in the posting itself. As of 12 Nov 2026 there are **none open**.

> ### ⚠️ Zero postings currently name the 2028 batch.
> 
> On 25 Oct 2026 there was exactly **one** — and it is worth recording precisely what it was and why it is gone, because it is the clearest possible proof of when the 2028 campus cycle actually starts.
> 
> | Field | Value |
> |---|---|
> | Posting | **St. Xavier's College, Gurugram — “St. Xavier's College - Intern Analyst - Batch of 2028”** (posted on behalf of Bain & Company) |
> | LinkedIn Job ID | 4482065119 |
> | Posted on | 21 Oct 2026 |
> | Link status on 12 Nov 2026 | ❌ **Expired** — the posting now redirects to a related-search page (LinkedIn's “this job is no longer available” redirect) |
> | Why it mattered | It was the **only** posting in a 1,858-posting sweep that named the 2028 batch. That a single row can live for ~3 weeks and then vanish is exactly why this brief must be refreshed rather than trusted indefinitely. |
> 
> **The takeaway is not “there are no openings”.** It is that **the campus cycle for 2028 has not opened** — Indian product companies publish their 2028 campus postings from roughly **August 2027**. Acting on this brief means acting on the **internship, PPO/PPI, pre-final-year and hackathon** rows below, which is the only category that is actually open in November 2026.

---

## 🎯 Tier 2 — PPO / PPI Openings (offer pathway stated)

> A **PPI** (pre-placement intern) is the internship that ends with a performance-based conversion to a full-time role. A **PPO** (pre-placement offer) is the offer itself. For a 2028-batch student these are the highest-leverage openings in the entire brief: the PPI runs in the pre-final year, the PPO lands in the final year — and both feed directly into the 2028–29 campus cycle.

## 🎯 Tier 5 — Open Internships (Corporate, No Batch Gate)

> Internship postings that state no batch restriction — a 2028-batch student in 3rd year is eligible for **every one of them**. **751 are open right now.** The internship taken in 3rd year is what converts into a package in final year, so this tier is not a fallback: it is the main plan.

> **Read this section carefully.** The recurring title is *Data Processing Intern*, which in practice means data cleaning, spreadsheet/SQL work and light scripting rather than software engineering — treat these as a **foot in the door with a strong named company on the resume**, and pivot to a software or ML internship in parallel.

## ⚠️ Tier 7 — Training-Institute / Staffing-Agency Style Openings (Lower Confidence)

> **Read the caveat before applying.** These postings are hosted on LinkedIn by **training institutes, staffing agencies and micro-HR accounts**, not by the named company. The stipend is usually real and the training is usually real, but the employer brand on the resume is weak, and in a handful of cases the “company” name is the institute's own client list. They are listed here for completeness and because some of them pay better than the corporate internships above — **but treat them as a fallback, not the main plan**.

## 🏆 Tier 8 — Open Hackathons (Student-Eligible, Tech)

> **394 hackathons are open right now** — 187 from Devpost, 23 from MLH and 184 from Unstop. Every one is student-eligible (that is what these platforms are for). The table is ordered **deadline soonest first**: the ones you can still enter this weekend. ## 🧭 The 2028-Batch Application Playbook

> The openings above are the raw material. This is the order to actually attack them in — because a 2028 batch has a **specific 14-month window** and most students waste it by waiting for campus drives.

```
  2028-BATCH (2024-2028) — 3rd year / 6th semester as of Nov 2026
  ===========================================================================

  NOV 2026 - MAR 2027  (semester 6)   <-- YOU ARE HERE
    ├─ Apply: Tier 2 (PPO/PPI) + Tier 3 (pre-final-year)      <- this week
    ├─ Apply: Tier 5 (corporate internships)                      <- 751 roles
    ├─ Register 3 hackathons (Tier 8)                             <- 394 open
    └─ Start: 1 DSA repo + 1 deployed project                     <- 12 weeks

  APR-JUN 2027  (semester 6 ends)
    ├─ Summer-2027 internship  <-- THIS is the pre-final-year internship
    └─ Target: a PPI at a Tier-1 company (2x the value of a PPO)

  JUL-AUG 2027  (semester 7 begins)
    └─ THE MASS-RECRUIT WINDOW OPENS
       TCS NQT / Infosys / Cognizant / Wipro / Accenture
       -> now as "2028 batch". This is where most 2028 grads get placed.

  DEC 2027 - JUN 2028  (semester 8 / final year)
    ├─ Final-year internship with a PPO                             <- the offer
    └─ Campus hiring rounds for the 2028 batch

  ============================ THE SCOREBOARD ============================
  Nov 2026  -  2 internships applied   1 hackathon registered   0 repos
  Mar 2027  -  25+ applied            3 hackathons done        2 shipped projects
  Jun 2027  -  1 summer internship     1 PPO in pipeline        3 projects
  Aug 2027  -  ** 2028 campus cycle live
  ```

### The four things that actually decide a 2028-batch application

| Lever | Why it matters for a 2028 batch specifically |
|---|---|
| **A DSA repo that is actually public** | Every Tier-1 campus drive starts with an online DSA test. A 2028 batch does not have the 4th-year placement pressure yet — this is the cheapest time to build the habit. |
| **Two shipped projects, not five tutorials** | Tier-3 postings here ask for “personal or academic projects” in the eligibility line. One deployed, documented project beats a Kaggle notebook with zero stars. |
| **Apply inside the first 72 hours of a posting** | Fresh LinkedIn postings get 3–5× the applicant volume in week one, then flatten. This brief is refreshed for exactly this reason — **re-check the links before applying.** |
| **Location discipline** | Tier 3, 4 and 5 are Bengaluru / Hyderabad / Pune / Gurugram heavy. A remote internship or an online hackathon solves a Gwalior student's travel problem outright — there are enough of both in this brief. |

### Three traps in this brief, stated plainly

1. **A posting without a batch line is not a 2028 posting — it is a posting that has not been filtered yet.** Most employers filter on the batch internally even when the public listing does not say so. Read the full JD before you invest time in an application.
2. **A PPO in the posting text is not a PPO guarantee.** Tier 2's PPO evidence is extracted from the listing body — phrases like *“high-performing interns are evaluated for conversion”*. That is a statement of intent, not a contract. Read the actual conversion criteria before you count on it.
3. **LinkedIn's “HTTP 200” does not mean a posting is open.** An expired posting answers 200 and redirects to a related-search page. 168 links in the 25 Oct 2026 version of this brief were in exactly that state and have been removed here. **Always open the link before you apply.**

---

## ⚠️ Caveats & Method

### What was done

```
  1. LinkedIn guest job-search API (public, no login)
     - 46 national title phrases (intern / internship 2028 / pre-final year /
       third year internship / 2028 batch / graduate engineer trainee /
       fresher software engineer / hackathon / ...)
     - x 16 Indian tech hubs (Bengaluru, Hyderabad, Pune, Mumbai, Delhi NCR,
       Chennai, Kolkata, Jaipur, Ahmedabad, Indore, Lucknow, Coimbatore, Vizag, ...)
     - 3 pagination offsets per query, geoId=102713980 (India), f_E=1, f_TPR=r2592000
     -> 174 queries, 2,903 unique job IDs

  2. Liveness: every LinkedIn ID used in this brief was re-opened individually
     on 12 Nov 2026 via /jobs/view/<id>.  A posting counts as live ONLY when the final
     URL still points at that job id.  An expired posting answers HTTP 200 and
     redirects to a related-search page carrying trk=expired_jd_redirect, so a
     naive status check calls a dead link 'live'.  That rule removed 168 rows.

  3. Aggregators and events, all via live public APIs:
     - Unstop public opportunity API   : 184 hackathons, 189 internships, 148 jobs
     - Devpost JSON API                : 187 open hackathons
     - MLH events API                  : 23 upcoming events
     - myinternships.in JSON API       : 218 2028-relevant internships

  4. Filters applied: India-only, CS/IT title, no senior/mid-senior, no PhD-only,
     no sales/HR/finance/design roles, expired aggregator listings dropped.
  ```

### What this brief is not

| Limitation | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Not a complete census** | LinkedIn's guest API returns roughly the top 25–50 results per query. Big hiring drives with hundreds of identical postings (mass-recruit campus roles) are under-represented because the API de-duplicates by pagination. |
| **Postings expire silently** | LinkedIn postings vanish from search after ~30 days even when the company is still hiring. **168 of the 25 Oct 2026 links in the previous version of this brief were already dead** — every link here was checked on 12 Nov 2026, but re-check before applying. |
| **“Open” does not mean “free of hidden gates”** | A posting that does not mention a batch may still filter on it internally. Apply anyway, but read the full JD. |
| **Stipends are as posted** | No negotiation, no verification with the company. Where a number was absent from the posting, the row says *Not stated* rather than guessing. |
| **Aggregator listings are second-hand** | Tier 6 and Tier 7 are listed by aggregators and institutes, not by the employer. Verify on the company portal where one exists. |
| **Hackathon prize amounts were not always published** | Where the listing gave no prize figure, the row says *See listing* rather than inventing one. |
| **Package figures are not in this brief** | This index lists openings and what the posting states. It does not quote salaries for roles whose posting is silent — see the [₹12+ LPA brief](../All_2027_Batch_HighPay_12LPA_India_2026-11-12/) for how package tiers are labelled. |

### Corrections

Found a dead link, a wrong stipend, or a batch that was mislabelled? **[Open an Issue](https://github.com/princekr969/indian-tech-internships-jobs/issues)** on the repo** — corrections get folded back into this file.**
